And for group2 {9,12}, possible values: 9+12=21, 12-9=3, 9*12=108, 9/12=0.75, 12/9=4/3 ≈1.333. We want to combine one value from group1 and one from group2 to get 24. Possibilities:
- If group1 gives 9 and group2 gives 3, then 9 * 3 = 27, not 24; 9 + 3 = 12, 9 - 3 = 6, 9/3 = 3.
- If group1 gives 10 and group2 gives 3, then 10 * 3 = 30, 10 + 3 = 13, 10 - 3 = 7, 10/3 ≈ 3.33.
- If group1 gives 11 and group2 gives 3, then 11 * 3 = 33, 11 + 3 = 14, 11 - 3 = 8, 11/3 ≈ 3.667.
- If group1 gives 9 and group2 gives 21, then 9 + 21 = 30, 9 - 21 = -12, 9 * 21 = 189, 9/21 = 3/7 ≈0.429.
- If group1 gives 10 and group2 gives 21, then 10+21=31, 10-21=-11, 10*21=210, 10/21≈0.476.
- If group1 gives 11 and group2 gives 21, then 11+21=32, 11-21=-10, 11*21=231, 11/21≈0.524.
- If group1 gives 9 and group2 gives 0.75, then 9/0.75 = 12, not 24; 9+0.75=9.75; 9-0.75=8.25; 9*0.75=6.75.
- If group1 gives 10 and group2 gives 0.75, then 10/0.75 = 13.33; 10+0.75=10.75; 10-0.75=9.25; 10*0.75=7.5.
- If group1 gives 11 and group2 gives 0.75, then 11/0.75 ≈14.67; 11+0.75=11.75; 11-0.75=10.25; 11*0.75=8.25.
- If group1 gives 9 and group2 gives 1.333, then 9/1.333 ≈6.75; 9+1.333=10.333; 9-1.333=7.667; 9*1.333≈12; 9/1.333 = 6.75.
- If group1 gives 10 and group2 gives 1.333, then 10/1.333 ≈7.5; 10+1.333=11.333; 10-1.333=8.667; 10*1.333=13.33.
- If group1 gives 11 and group2 gives 1.333, then 11/1.333 ≈8.25; 11+1.333=12.333; 11-1.333=9.667; 11*1.333=14.667.
- If group1 gives 10 and group2 gives 108, then 10+108=118; 10-108=-98; 10*108=1080; 10/108≈0.0926; 108/10=10.8.
- If group1 gives 9 and group2 gives 108, then 9+108=117; 9-108=-99; 9*108=972; 9/108=0.0833; 108/9=12.
- If group1 gives 11 and group2 gives 108, then 11+108=119; 11-108=-97; 11*108=1188; 11/108≈0.102; 108/11≈9.818.
None of these give 24.

Now try partition: Group1: {1,12} and Group2: {9,10}.

For group1 {1,12}:
Possible expressions: 1+12=13, 12-1=11, 1*12=12, 12/1=12, 1/12.
So possible values: 13, 11, 12, 1/12.
For group2 {9,10}:
Possible expressions: 9+10=19, 10-9=1, 9-10=-1, 9*10=90, 10*9=90, 9/10=0.9, 10/9≈1.111.
So possible values: 19, 1, -1, 90, 0.9, 1.111.
Now combine these:
Maybe 12 (from group1) and 2 (but 2 is not available from group2; we have 1, 19, -1, 90, 0.9, 1.111).
